# Français (fr): reviewed translations

Every entry of `locale/fr/LC_MESSAGES/strings.po` that this change replaces. "Old" is the translation in the repository before this change (marked *fuzzy* when the program did not use it), "New" the reviewed one. Approve or correct each row in `fr.csv` (columns *Approved* and *Translator note*). See [README.md](README.md) for how the changes were made.

|---|---|

| corrected | 693 |
| fuzzy corrected | 333 |
| fuzzy confirmed | 8 |
| untranslated copy | 6 |
| new | 258 |
| manual | 1 |
| **Total** | **1305** |

| 295 | corrected | Cut Path | Coupé Piste | Couper le chemin | 'Coupé Piste' is ungrammatical (past participle) and 'piste' means a copper track. The Geometry Editor tool cuts a shape along a drawn path (glossary: Path = 'Chemin'). — reviewer: The old text was wrong, but 'selon un chemin' (along a path) suggests a path is the cutter. In appGeoEditor.cutpath() the first selected shape is turned into its outline path and cut by the other selected shapes. A neutral 'Couper le chemin' is faithful, and the other translations (de 'Pfad ausschneiden', it 'Taglia Percorso') read the same way. Uses the glossary terms Cut = 'Couper', Path = 'Chemin'. | ☐ |
